# Third-party notices

Keg includes software written by others. Each part is listed here with
its licence; those licences, not the Keg License, apply to those parts.
The full texts the licences ask to be passed on are at the end.

This file is generated by `Scripts/third-party-notices.sh`. Do not edit it by hand.

## Summary

| Part of Keg | What it contains by others | Licences |
|---|---|---|
| `keg`, `kegd`, `keg-agent` | 198 Rust crates, and the Rust standard library | MIT (167), Unicode-3.0 (21), Apache-2.0 (4), BSD-3-Clause (4), ISC (3), CDLA-Permissive-2.0 (2), Zlib (1), BSD-2-Clause (1) |
| The Mac app | sparkle 2.10.1, swiftterm 1.11.2 | MIT |
| The Linux machine (on a Mac) | The Linux kernel 6.18.35 | GPL-2.0-only |
| The Linux machine (on a Mac) | 71 packages of Alpine Linux 3.24.4, 29 of them (by source) under the GPL or LGPL | see the list below |
| `keg`, `kegd` | tiny_http 0.12.0, changed by Keg | MIT OR Apache-2.0 |

## Source code

The Linux machine Keg runs on a Mac holds a Linux kernel and programs and
libraries under the GNU General Public License or the GNU Lesser General
Public License. The complete corresponding source for the GPL and LGPL
parts of this release is published with it, at
https://github.com/rahult/keg/releases/tag/v0.10.0, in the files:

- `keg-0.10.0-gpl-source-kernel.tar.xz`: the Linux kernel's source release,
  the patches and the configuration it was built with, and the scripts that
  built it;
- `keg-0.10.0-gpl-source-packages.tar` (with `-2.tar` and on, if there are
  more): each Alpine package with a GPL or LGPL part, as its build recipe at
  the commit it was built from, with every source archive that recipe names.

Each has a README, which says how its parts match the files of the Linux
machine and how to build them again, and a MANIFEST.txt with the SHA-256
of every file.

Keg's own programs (`keg`, `kegd`, `keg-agent`, `keg-vm` and the app) are
separate works that run on and beside this software. In the Linux
machine, `kegd`, `keg-agent` and `keg` are linked statically with musl
(MIT), the Rust standard library and the crates listed below, and with no
GPL or LGPL library; they run there as separate programs. On a Mac, `keg`,
`keg-vm` and the app are linked with macOS's own libraries and the Swift
packages listed below.

## The Rust crates in `keg`, `kegd` and `keg-agent`

`keg-agent` (on Linux, and in the Linux machine Keg runs on a Mac).
Where a crate may be used under one of several licences, Keg uses it
under the one in the last column.

### The Rust standard library

Every Rust program carries parts of the Rust standard library, from the
Rust Keg is built with (`rust-toolchain.toml`). It is under MIT OR
Apache-2.0; some of its parts are under other permissive licences, each
named in the file COPYRIGHT-library that comes with Rust
(`share/doc/rust/COPYRIGHT-library.html`). Keg uses it under MIT:

```text
MIT License

Copyright (c) <year> <copyright holders>

Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy of this software and associated documentation files (the "Software"), to deal in the Software without restriction, including without limitation the rights to use, copy, modify, merge, publish, distribute, sublicense, and/or sell copies of the Software, and to permit persons to whom the Software is furnished to do so, subject to the following conditions:

The above copyright notice and this permission notice shall be included in all copies or substantial portions of the Software.

THE SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED "AS IS", WITHOUT WARRANTY OF ANY KIND, EXPRESS OR IMPLIED, INCLUDING BUT NOT LIMITED TO THE WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY, FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE AND NONINFRINGEMENT. IN NO EVENT SHALL THE AUTHORS OR COPYRIGHT HOLDERS BE LIABLE FOR ANY CLAIM, DAMAGES OR OTHER LIABILITY, WHETHER IN AN ACTION OF CONTRACT, TORT OR OTHERWISE, ARISING FROM, OUT OF OR IN CONNECTION WITH THE SOFTWARE OR THE USE OR OTHER DEALINGS IN THE SOFTWARE.
```

### tiny_http, changed by Keg

`keg` and `kegd` contain tiny_http 0.12.0 (MIT OR Apache-2.0), by
Pierre Krieger and its other authors, with changes made by Keg: an
unread request body is no longer read into one buffer as large as the
request says, and a request's lines are limited. The changed copy, with
its licences and the whole difference from the release (`keg.patch`),
is `crates/vendor/tiny_http` in Keg's source. It is used under MIT; the
text is with the other crates' texts below.

## The Mac app's Swift packages

| Package | Version | Licence | Where from | In the app |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| sparkle | 2.10.1 | MIT | https://github.com/sparkle-project/Sparkle.git | yes, [text](#text-e25c68cce7) |
| swift-argument-parser | 1.8.2 | Apache-2.0 | https://github.com/apple/swift-argument-parser | no: only a tool of SwiftTerm's uses it |
| swiftterm | 1.11.2 | MIT | https://github.com/migueldeicaza/SwiftTerm.git | yes, [text](#text-369a18d06e) |

Sparkle's licence includes the notices of bsdiff, sais-lite and the
other code it contains; they are reproduced with it.

## The Linux machine Keg runs on a Mac

On a Mac, Keg runs containers in a small Linux machine. Its files come in
the app (`Contents/SharedSupport/vm`) and in the macOS archive (`vm/`).
`keg-vm`, which starts it, is Keg's own.

### The Linux kernel

- File: `vmlinux`, `vmlinux-6.18.35-197-debug` as published by Kata Containers, sha256 `fb2cfb79eb1ae19447a85d75682d7fa5cfec97e24beb2609a492b806e8072c8d`.
- Version: Linux 6.18.35, built with gcc (Ubuntu 11.4.0-1ubuntu1~22.04.3) 11.4.0, GNU ld (GNU Binutils for Ubuntu) 2.38.
- Where the binary comes from: https://github.com/kata-containers/kata-containers/releases/download/3.32.0/kata-static-3.32.0-arm64.tar.zst (the file `opt/kata/share/kata-containers/vmlinux-6.18.35-197-debug`).
- Licence: GPL-2.0-only, with the Linux syscall note.
- Source: Linux 6.18.35, https://cdn.kernel.org/pub/linux/kernel/v6.x/linux-6.18.35.tar.xz,
  built by Kata Containers' recipe at release 3.32.0: the configuration fragments,
  patches and build script in https://github.com/kata-containers/kata-containers/tree/3.32.0/tools/packaging/kernel.
- Configuration: the exact configuration is inside the kernel file itself (CONFIG_IKCONFIG);
  Linux's `scripts/extract-ikconfig vmlinux` prints it.

### Alpine Linux 3.24.4

The machine's programs and libraries are packages of Alpine Linux, as
installed in the image Keg builds them in. Each package's source is its
build recipe in Alpine's aports repository at the commit named, with the
source archives that recipe names (also kept at
https://distfiles.alpinelinux.org/distfiles/v3.24/).
Packages Keg itself uses there include busybox (the shell and basic
commands), musl (the C library), e2fsprogs (mkfs.ext4), iptables and
iproute2 (networks), util-linux and socat. The rest are there because
they were in the image Keg's programs were built in.

1. You may make and give away verbatim copies of the source form of the Standard Version of this Package without restriction, provided that you duplicate all of the original copyright notices and associated disclaimers.

2. You may apply bug fixes, portability fixes and other modifications derived from the Public Domain or from the Copyright Holder.  A Package modified in such a way shall still be considered the Standard Version.

     c) rename any non-standard executables so the names do not conflict with standard executables, which must also be provided, and provide a separate manual page for each non-standard executable that clearly documents how it differs from the Standard Version.
     d) make other distribution arrangements with the Copyright Holder.

4. You may distribute the programs of this Package in object code or executable form, provided that you do at least ONE of the following:

If such scripts or library files are aggregated with this Package via the so-called "undump" or "unexec" methods of producing a binary executable image, then distribution of such an image shall neither be construed as a distribution of this Package nor shall it fall under the restrictions of Paragraphs 3 and 4, provided that you do not represent such an executable image as a Standard Version of this Package.
